{"forms":[{"form":"brown bags","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"brown bagging","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"brown bagged","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"brown bagged","tags":["past"]},{"form":"brown-bag","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I'm brown bagging it again this month because the combination of healthy plus inexpensive is hard to beat otherwise.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To carry one's packed lunch from home, either literally or figuratively in a brown paper bag or a lunchbox, rather than purchase it from a cafeteria or restaurant."],"tags":["ambitransitive","informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I'm brown bagging it again today because I haven't managed to kick this habit yet.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To consume alcohol in public from the bottle or can, while it is held concealed inside a brown paper bag, so as to avoid attention e.g. from the police."],"tags":["ambitransitive","informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I'm brown bagging it again this year because I don't need to be spending money on expensive hybrids.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To plant seed saved from one's own harvest, as opposed to buying new seed from a seed company."],"tags":["ambitransitive","informal"],"topics":["agriculture","business","lifestyle"]}],"sounds":[],"upstream_line":413532,"upstream_offset":1969067926,"upstream_record_sha256":"e5506c2f609402169f48a5ab0a55f492b9931db7adbf1f6924f6e8d08561d9d0","word":"brown bag"}
